### 创建文件：`/root/geosafe_vla/scripts/run_phase2_phase3.sh`

设计一个 bash 脚本，一键执行所有 Phase 2 + Phase 3 分析步骤。

#### 脚本结构

```bash
#!/bin/bash
# run_phase2_phase3.sh — D005 Post-Eval Analysis Pipeline
# 输入: baseline 和 geosafe 的 eval 结果目录
# 输出: /root/geosafe_vla/results/d005_summary.md + figures + tables
#
# Usage:
#   bash run_phase2_phase3.sh &lt;baseline_results_dir> &lt;geosafe_results_dir> [gpu_serve] [gpu_eval]
#
# Example:
#   bash run_phase2_phase3.sh \
#     /root/geosafe_vla/results/baseline_v3_s1 \
#     /root/geosafe_vla/results/geosafe_v3_s1 \
#     0 1

set -euo pipefail  # 但每个子步骤用 || true 容错

BASELINE_DIR="${1:?Usage: $0 &lt;baseline_dir> &lt;geosafe_dir> [gpu_serve] [gpu_eval]}"
GEOSAFE_DIR="${2:?Usage: $0 &lt;baseline_dir> &lt;geosafe_dir> [gpu_serve] [gpu_eval]}"
GPU_SERVE="${3:-0}"
GPU_EVAL="${4:-1}"
RESULTS_DIR="/root/geosafe_vla/results"
SUMMARY_FILE="$RESULTS_DIR/d005_summary.md"
SCRIPTS_DIR="/root/geosafe_vla/scripts"
CHECKPOINT_BASE="/root/autodl-tmp/checkpoints/pi05_libero"

# 激活环境
source /root/miniconda3/etc/profile.d/conda.sh && conda activate base

echo "============================================"
echo "D005 Phase 2+3 Analysis Pipeline"
echo "Started: $(date -u '+%Y-%m-%d %H:%M:%S UTC')"
echo "Baseline: $BASELINE_DIR"
echo "GeoSafe:  $GEOSAFE_DIR"
echo "GPU:      serve=$GPU_SERVE, eval=$GPU_EVAL"
echo "============================================"

# 初始化 summary
cat > "$SUMMARY_FILE" << 'HEADER'
# D005 Summary Report
> Auto-generated by run_phase2_phase3.sh

HEADER

ERRORS=0

run_step() {
    local step_name="$1"
    shift
    echo ""
    echo ">>> [$step_name] Starting..."
    if "$@"; then
        echo ">>> [$step_name] ✅ Done"
    else
        echo ">>> [$step_name] ❌ FAILED (exit=$?)"
        echo "- ❌ $step_name: FAILED" >> "$SUMMARY_FILE"
        ((ERRORS++))
    fi
}
```

- 计算 ΔCAR = geosafe_overall_CAR - baseline_overall_CAR
- 计算 ΔTSR = geosafe_overall_TSR - baseline_overall_TSR
- 判定场景：
  ```
  if ΔCAR >= 20 and ΔTSR >= -5:  → S1 (strong positive)
  elif ΔCAR >= 10:                → S2 (weak positive)  
  elif ΔCAR >= 5:                 → S3 (marginal)
  else:                           → S4 (negative)
  ```
- 输出建议使用的 Abstract 版本 (abstract_scenario_1_strong.txt 等)
- 输出建议的下一步行动

```bash
run_step "Phase 3.4: Scenario Judgment" python "$SCRIPTS_DIR/judge_scenario.py" \
    --metrics "$RESULTS_DIR/aggregate_metrics.json" \
    | tee -a "$SUMMARY_FILE"
```

**3.5 生成 Figure** — 调用已有 plot 脚本

```bash
run_step "Phase 3.5: SafeLIBERO Bar Chart" python "$SCRIPTS_DIR/plot_safelibero_results.py" \
    --baseline_dir "$BASELINE_DIR" \
    --geosafe_dir "$GEOSAFE_DIR" \
    --output "$RESULTS_DIR/figures/safelibero_results.pdf"
```

#### 尾部汇总

```bash
echo ""
echo "============================================"
echo "Pipeline completed with $ERRORS error(s)"
echo "Summary: $SUMMARY_FILE"
echo "============================================"

# 追加完成信息到 summary
echo "" >> "$SUMMARY_FILE"
echo "---" >> "$SUMMARY_FILE"
echo "Generated: $(date -u '+%Y-%m-%d %H:%M:%S UTC')" >> "$SUMMARY_FILE"
echo "Errors: $ERRORS" >> "$SUMMARY_FILE"
```

### 额外创建的 Python 脚本

你需要创建以下新脚本（上面引用的）：

1. **`measure_inference_latency.py`** — inference latency 测量
2. **`aggregate_safelibero_results.py`** — 汇总 eval 结果 + AEGIS 对比
3. **`extract_and_plot_trajectories.py`** — 轨迹可视化（简化版，graceful fallback）
4. **`judge_scenario.py`** — 场景判定 + Abstract 推荐

每个脚本的文件头注释说明：这个脚本做什么、输入输出、关键依赖。

### 验证

创建完所有脚本后：
1. `chmod +x /root/geosafe_vla/scripts/run_phase2_phase3.sh`
2. 检查每个 Python 脚本语法：`python -c "import ast; ast.parse(open('script.py').read()); print('OK')"` 
3. 列出所有创建的文件及大小
4. `bash run_phase2_phase3.sh --help` 或类似方式确认脚本可解析

### 重要注意

- 不要运行 eval 或启动任何 GPU 进程——只创建脚本
- 脚本要处理 eval 结果 JSON 可能的格式：检查 `$BASELINE_DIR` 下已有的 JSON 文件结构（如果有的话），以正确解析
- 先 `ls` 查看已有结果文件的格式再写解析代码
- 每个 Python 脚本都用 argparse，支持 `--help`
- 所有路径参数化，不硬编码

{"result":"{\"success\": true, \"data\": {\"stdout\": \"\\\"\\\"\\\"Bootstrap confidence interval calculator for SafeLIBERO evaluation results.\\n\\nComputes per-task, per-suite, and overall bootstrap CIs for TSR, CAR, and\\nSafety Score (SS = TSR * CAR) from SafeLIBERO evaluation JSON outputs.\\n\\nSupports two input formats:\\n  1. Batch eval JSON (safelibero_results_*_level*.json) — per-task aggregated rates\\n  2. Runtime SDF eval JSON (runtime_sdf_*.json) — per-episode binary outcomes\\n\\\"\\\"\\\"\\n\\nimport argparse\\nimport json\\nimport sys\\nfrom pathlib import Path\\n\\nimport numpy as np\\n\\nSUITE_NAMES = [\\n    \\\"safelibero_spatial\\\", \\\"safelibero_object\\\",\\n    \\\"safelibero_goal\\\", \\\"safelibero_long\\\",\\n]\\nSUITE_SHORT = {\\n    \\\"safelibero_spatial\\\": \\\"Spatial\\\",\\n    \\\"safelibero_object\\\": \\\"Object\\\",\\n    \\\"safelibero_goal\\\": \\\"Goal\\\",\\n    \\\"safelibero_long\\\": \\\"Long\\\",\\n}\\nTASKS_PER_SUITE = 4\\nDEFAULT_EPISODES = 50\\n\\n\\ndef parse_args():\\n    p = argparse.ArgumentParser(\\n        description=\\\"Bootstrap confidence intervals for SafeLIBERO evaluation\\\",\\n        formatter_class=argparse.ArgumentDefaultsHelpFormatter,\\n    )\\n    p.add_argument(\\\"--input\\\", type=str, nargs=\\\"+\\\",\\n                   help=\\\"Path(s) to eval result JSON file(s)\\\")\\n    p.add_argument(\\\"--n-bootstrap\\\", type=int, default=10000,\\n                   help=\\\"Number of bootstrap resampling iterations\\\")\\n    p.add_argument(\\\"--ci-level\\\", type=float, default=0.95,\\n                   help=\\\"Confidence interval level\\\")\\n    p.add_argument(\\\"--seed\\\", type=int, default=42, help=\\\"Random seed\\\")\\n    p.add_argument(\\\"--format\\\", choices=[\\\"table\\\", \\\"latex\\\", \\\"json\\\", \\\"all\\\"],\\n                   default=\\\"table\\\", help=\\\"Output format\\\")\\n    p.add_argument(\\\"--output\\\", type=str, help=\\\"Output file path (optional)\\\")\\n    p.add_argument(\\\"--demo\\\", action=\\\"store_true\\\",\\n                   help=\\\"Generate demo data and run bootstrap\\\")\\n    return p.parse_args()\\n\\n\\n# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\\n# Data loading\\n# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\\n\\ndef _reconstruct_episodes(tsr, car, n):\\n    # Fallback: assumes independence between TSR and CAR (per-episode pairing unavailable)\\n    n_success = round(tsr * n)\\n    n_collision = round((1 - car) * n)\\n    success = np.array([1] * n_success + [0] * (n - n_success), dtype=np.int32)\\n    collision = np.array([1] * n_collision + [0] * (n - n_collision), dtype=np.int32)\\n    return success, collision\\n\\n\\ndef load_batch_eval(data, num_episodes=DEFAULT_EPISODES):\\n    \\\"\\\"\\\"Load from batch eval format: {suites: {suite: {tasks: {task_X: {tsr, car}}}}}.\\\"\\\"\\\"\\n    tasks = {}\\n    has_paired = 0\\n    has_unpaired = 0\\n    for suite in SUITE_NAMES:\\n        suite_data = data.get(\\\"suites\\\", {}).get(suite, {})\\n        for tk, tv in suite_data.get(\\\"tasks\\\", {}).items():\\n            if \\\"error\\\" in tv:\\n                continue\\n            if \\\"episodes\\\" in tv and tv[\\\"episodes\\\"]:\\n                episodes = tv[\\\"episodes\\\"]\\n                success = np.array([int(ep[\\\"success\\\"]) for ep in episodes], dtype=np.int32)\\n                collision = np.array([int(ep[\\\"collision\\\"]) for ep in episodes], dtype=np.int32)\\n                has_paired += 1\\n            else:\\n\", \"stderr\": \"\", \"return_code\": 0}, \"error\": null, \"metadata\": {\"elapsed_seconds\": 0.45}}"}

       return json.load(f)\\n\\n\\ndef demo_data():\\n    random.seed(42)\\n    suites = [\\\"Spatial\\\", \\\"Object\\\", \\\"Goal\\\", \\\"Long\\\"]\\n    tsr_bl = [[random.uniform(60, 80), random.uniform(55, 75)] for _ in suites]\\n    tsr_gs = [[random.uniform(55, 80), random.uniform(50, 75)] for _ in suites]\\n    car_bl = [[random.uniform(15, 30), random.uniform(10, 25)] for _ in suites]\\n    car_gs = [[random.uniform(40, 70), random.uniform(35, 65)] for _ in suites]\\n    return {\\n        \\\"methods\\\": [\\\"baseline\\\", \\\"geosafe\\\"],\\n        \\\"suites\\\": suites,\\n        \\\"levels\\\": [1, 2],\\n        \\\"tsr\\\": {\\\"baseline\\\": tsr_bl, \\\"geosafe\\\": tsr_gs},\\n        \\\"car\\\": {\\\"baseline\\\": car_bl, \\\"geosafe\\\": car_gs},\\n    }\\n\\n\\ndef plot(data, output_dir, output_name, show_car=True):\\n    plt.rcParams.update({\\n        \\\"font.family\\\": \\\"serif\\\",\\n        \\\"font.size\\\": 8,\\n bar_width = 0.35\\n    gap_within = 0.15\\n    gap_between = 0.8\\n\\n    x = []\\n    pos = 0.0\\n    for si in range(n_suites):\\n        for li in range(n_levels):\\n            x.append(pos)\\n            pos += 1.0 + gap_within\\n        pos += gap_between - gap_within\\n    x = np.array(x)\\n\\n    bars1 = ax.bar(x - bar_width / 2, tsr_baseline, bar_width,\\n ── Statistics ───────────────────────────────────────────────────\\n        arr = np.array(latencies)\\n        stats = {\\n            \\\"mean\\\": float(np.mean(arr)),\\n            \\\"std\\\": float(np.std(arr)),\\n            \\\"median\\\": float(np.median(arr)),\\n            \\\"p95\\\": float(np.percentile(arr, 95)),\\n            \\\"p99\\\": float(np.percentile(arr, 99)),\\n        }\\n\\n        output = {\\n            \\\"checkpoint\\\": args.checkpoint,\\n            \\\"mode\\\": args.mode,\\n            \\\"num_requests\\\": args.num_requests,\\n            \\\"warmup\\\": args.warmup,\\n            \\\"latency_ms\\\": stats,\\n            \\\"peak_gpu_memory_gb\\\": round(peak_mem_gb, 2),\\n            \\\"gpu_model\\\": gpu_name,\\n            \\\"timestamp\\\": time.strftime(\\\"%Y-%m-%dT%H:%M:%S\\\"),\\n        }\\n\\n        out_path = pathlib.Path(args.output)\\n        out_path.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)\\n        with open(out_path, \\\"w\\\") as f:\\n            json.dump(output, f, indent=2)\\n\\n        print(f\\\"\\\\n{'='*50}\\\")\\n        print(f\\\"Mode:       {args.mode}\\\")\\n        print(f\\\"Requests:   {args.num_requests} (warmup: {args.warmup})\\\")\\n        print(f\\\"Latency:    mean={stats['mean']:.1f}ms  std={stats['std']:.1f}ms  \\\"\\n              f\\\"median={stats['median']:.1f}ms  p95={stats['p95']:.1f}ms  p99={stats['p99']:.1f}ms\\\")\\n        print(f\\\"GPU mem:    {peak_mem_gb:.2f} GB ({gpu_name})\\\")\\n        print(f\\\"Saved:      {out_path}\\\")\\n        print(f\\\"{'='*50}\\\")\\n\\n    finally:\\n        cleanup()\\n\\n\\nif __name__ == \\\"__main__\\\":\\n    main()\\n\", \"stderr\": \"\", \"return_code\": 0}, \"error\": null, \"metadata\": {\"elapsed_seconds\": 0.5}}"

{"result":"{\"success\": true, \"data\": {\"stdout\": \"#!/usr/bin/env python3\\n# Learning curve plot for GeoSafe VLA paper.\\n# Usage:\\n#   python plot_learning_curve.py --demo                         # demo with mock data\\n#   python plot_learning_curve.py --input data.json              # from JSON\\n#   python plot_learning_curve.py --input data.csv --no-tsr      # CSV, CAR only\\n\\nimport argparse\\nimport json\\nimport csv\\nimport os\\nimport matplotlib\\nmatplotlib.use(\\\"Agg\\\")\\nimport matplotlib.pyplot as plt\\nfrom matplotlib.ticker import FuncFormatter\\n\\ndef load_json(path):\\n    with open(path) as f:\\n        return json.load(f)\\n\\ndef load_csv(path):\\n    data = {\\\"steps\\\": [], \\\"baseline\\\": {\\\"car\\\": [], \\\"tsr\\\": []}, \\\"geosafe\\\": {\\\"car\\\": [], \\\"tsr\\\": []}}\\n    with open(path) as f:\\n        reader = csv.DictReader(f)\\n        for row in reader:\\n            data[\\\"steps\\\"].append(int(row[\\\"step\\\"]))\\n            data[\\\"baseline\\\"][\\\"car\\\"].append(float(row[\\\"baseline_car\\\"]))\\n            data[\\\"geosafe\\\"][\\\"car\\\"].append(float(row[\\\"geosafe_car\\\"]))\\n            if \\\"baseline_tsr\\\" in row and \\\"geosafe_tsr\\\" in row:\\n                data[\\\"baseline\\\"][\\\"tsr\\\"].append(float(row[\\\"baseline_tsr\\\"]))\\n                data[\\\"geosafe\\\"][\\\"tsr\\\"].append(float(row[\\\"geosafe_tsr\\\"]))\\n    if not data[\\\"baseline\\\"][\\\"tsr\\\"]:\\n        del data[\\\"baseline\\\"][\\\"tsr\\\"]\\n        del data[\\\"geosafe\\\"][\\\"tsr\\\"]\\n    return data\\n\\ndef demo_data():\\n    return {\\n        \\\"steps\\\": [20000, 25000, 30000],\\n        \\\"baseline\\\": {\\\"car\\\": [51.8, 54.2, 57.6], \\\"tsr\\\": [47.5, 58.3, 64.0]},\\n        \\\"geosafe\\\": {\\\"car\\\": [64.1, 69.5, 74.8], \\\"tsr\\\": [46.2, 56.9, 62.5]},\\n    }\\n\\ndef fmt_steps(x, _):\\n    if x >= 1000:\\n        return f\\\"{int(x/1000)}K\\\"\\n    return str(int(x))\\n\\ndef plot(data, output_prefix, output_dir, show_tsr=True, title=None):\\n    plt.rcParams.update({\\n        \\\"font.family\\\": \\\"serif\\\",\\n        \\\"font.size\\\": 8,\\n        \\\"axes.labelsize\\\": 9,\\n        \\\"xtick.labelsize\\\": 8,\\n        \\\"ytick.labelsize\\\": 8,\\n        \\\"legend.fontsize\\\": 7,\\n        \\\"pdf.fonttype\\\": 42,\\n        \\\"ps.fonttype\\\": 42,\\n    })\\n\\n    fig, ax1 = plt.subplots(figsize=(3.25, 2.4))\\n\\n    steps = data[\\\"steps\\\"]\\n\\n    # CAR lines (primary Y axis)\\n    ax1.plot(steps, data[\\\"baseline\\\"][\\\"car\\\"], color=\\\"#2166ac\\\", linestyle=\\\"-\\\", linewidth=1.5,\\n             marker=\\\"o\\\", markersize=4, markerfacecolor=\\\"#2166ac\\\", markeredgecolor=\\\"white\\\",\\n             markeredgewidth=0.5, label=\\\"Baseline CAR\\\", zorder=3)\\n    ax1.plot(steps, data[\\\"geosafe\\\"][\\\"car\\\"], color=\\\"#b2182b\\\", linestyle=\\\"-\\\", linewidth=1.5,\\n             marker=\\\"s\\\", markersize=4, markerfacecolor=\\\"#b2182b\\\", markeredgecolor=\\\"white\\\",\\n             markeredgewidth=0.5, label=\\\"GeoSafe CAR\\\", zorder=3)\\n\\n    ax1.set_xlabel(\\\"Training Steps\\\")\\n    ax1.set_ylabel(\\\"CAR (%)\\\")\\n    ax1.set_ylim(0, 100)\\n    ax1.set_xticks(steps)\\n    ax1.xaxis.set_major_formatter(FuncFormatter(fmt_steps))\\n\\n    has_tsr = show_tsr and \\\"tsr\\\" in data.get(\\\"baseline\\\", {}) and \\\"tsr\\\" in data.get(\\\"geosafe\\\", {})\\n\\n    if has_tsr:\\n        ax2 = ax1.twinx()\\n        ax2.plot(steps, data[\\\"baseline\\\"][\\\"tsr\\\"], color=\\\"#2166ac\\\", linestyle=\\\"--\\\", linewidth=1.2,\\n                 marker=\\\"o\\\", markersize=3, markerfacecolor=\\\"none\\\", markeredgecolor=\\\"#2166ac\\\",\\n                 markeredgewidth=0.8, label=\\\"Baseline TSR\\\", zorder=2)\\n        ax2.plot(steps, data[\\\"geosafe\\\"][\\\"tsr\\\"], color=\\\"#b2182b\\\", linestyle=\\\"--\\\", linewidth=1.2,\\n                 marker=\\\"s\\\", markersize=3, markerfacecolor=\\\"none\\\", markeredgecolor=\\\"#b2182b\\\",\\n                 markeredgewidth=0.8, label=\\\"GeoSafe TSR\\\", zorder=2)\\n        ax2.set_ylabel(\\\"TSR (%)\\\")\\n        ax2.set_ylim(0, 100)\\n        ax2.tick_params(axis=\\\"y\\\", labelsize=8)\\n\\n    # Remove grid, keep spines clean\\n    ax1.grid(False)\\n    if has_tsr:\\n        ax2.grid(False)\\n\\n    # Combined legend\\n    lines1, labels1 = ax1.get_legend_handles_labels()\\n    if has_tsr:\\n        lines2, labels2 = ax2.get_legend_handles_labels()\\n        lines1 += lines2\\n        labels1 += labels2\\n    ax1.legend(lines1, labels1, loc=\\\"lower right\\\", frameon=True, framealpha=0.85,\\n               edgecolor=\\\"#cccccc\\\", fancybox=False)\\n\\n    if title:\\n        ax1.set_title(title, fontsize=9, pad=6)\\n\\n    fig.tight_layout()\\n\\n    os.makedirs(output_dir, exist_ok=True)\\n    pdf_path = os.path.join(output_dir, f\\\"{output_prefix}.pdf\\\")\\n    png_path = os.path.join(output_dir, f\\\"{output_prefix}.png\\\")\\n    fig.savefig(pdf_path, format=\\\"pdf\\\", bbox_inches=\\\"tight\\\")\\n    fig.savefig(png_path, format=\\\"png\\\", dpi=300, bbox_inches=\\\"tight\\\")\\n    plt.close(fig)\\n    print(f\\\"Saved: {pdf_path}\\\")\\n    print(f\\\"Saved: {png_path}\\\")\\n\\ndef main():\\n    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(description=\\\"Plot learning curves for GeoSafe VLA paper\\\")\\n    parser.add_argument(\\\"--input\\\", type=str, help=\\\"Path to JSON or CSV data file\\\")\\n    parser.add_argument(\\\"--output\\\", type=str, default=\\\"learning_curve\\\", help=\\\"Output filename prefix\\\")\\n    parser.add_argument(\\\"--output-dir\\\", type=str, default=\\\"/root/geosafe_vla/artifacts/\\\", help=\\\"Output directory\\\")\\n    parser.add_argument(\\\"--demo\\\", action=\\\"store_true\\\", help=\\\"Generate demo plot with mock data\\\")\\n    parser.add_argument(\\\"--no-tsr\\\", action=\\\"store_true\\\", help=\\\"Omit TSR curves\\\")\\n    parser.add_argument(\\\"--title\\\", type=str, default=None, help=\\\"Optional plot title\\\")\\n    args = parser.parse_args()\\n\\n    if args.demo:\\n        data = demo_data()\\n    elif args.input:\\n        if args.input.endswith(\\\".csv\\\"):\\n            data = load_csv(args.input)\\n        else:\\n            data = load_json(args.input)\\n    else:\\n        parser.error(\\\"Provide --input or --demo\\\")\\n\\n    plot(data, args.output, args.output_dir, show_tsr=not args.no_tsr, title=args.title)\\n\\nif __name__ == \\\"__main__\\\":\\n    main()\\n\", \"stderr\": \"\", \"return_code\": {"result":"{\"success\": true, \"data\": {\"stdout\": \"\\\"\\\"\\\"Bootstrap confidence interval calculator for SafeLIBERO evaluation results.\\n\\nComputes per-task, per-suite, and overall bootstrap CIs for TSR, CAR, and\\nSafety Score (SS = TSR * CAR) from SafeLIBERO evaluation JSON outputs.\\n\\nSupports two input formats:\\n  1. Batch eval JSON (safelibero_results_*_level*.json) — per-task aggregated rates\\n  2. Runtime SDF eval JSON (runtime_sdf_*.json) — per-episode binary outcomes\\n\\\"\\\"\\\"\\n\\nimport argparse\\nimport json\\nimport sys\\nfrom pathlib import Path\\n\\nimport numpy as np\\n\\nSUITE_NAMES = [\\n    \\\"safelibero_spatial\\\", \\\"safelibero_object\\\",\\n    \\\"safelibero_goal\\\", \\\"safelibero_long\\\",\\n]\\nSUITE_SHORT = {\\n    \\\"safelibero_spatial\\\": \\\"Spatial\\\",\\n    \\\"safelibero_object\\\": \\\"Object\\\",\\n    \\\"safelibero_goal\\\": \\\"Goal\\\",\\n    \\\"safelibero_long\\\": \\\"Long\\\",\\n}\\nTASKS_PER_SUITE = 4\\nDEFAULT_EPISODES = 50\\n\\n\\ndef parse_args():\\n    p = argparse.ArgumentParser(\\n        description=\\\"Bootstrap confidence intervals for SafeLIBERO evaluation\\\",\\n        formatter_class=argparse.ArgumentDefaultsHelpFormatter,\\n    )\\n    p.add_argument(\\\"--input\\\", type=str, nargs=\\\"+\\\",\\n                   help=\\\"Path(s) to eval result JSON file(s)\\\")\\n    p.add_argument(\\\"--n-bootstrap\\\", type=int, default=10000,\\n                   help=\\\"Number of bootstrap resampling iterations\\\")\\n    p.add_argument(\\\"--ci-level\\\", type=float, default=0.95,\\n                   help=\\\"Confidence interval level\\\")\\n    p.add_argument(\\\"--seed\\\", type=int, default=42, help=\\\"Random seed\\\")\\n    p.add_argument(\\\"--format\\\", choices=[\\\"table\\\", \\\"latex\\\", \\\"json\\\", \\\"all\\\"],\\n                   default=\\\"table\\\", help=\\\"Output format\\\")\\n    p.add_argument(\\\"--output\\\", type=str, help=\\\"Output file path (optional)\\\")\\n    p.add_argument(\\\"--demo\\\", action=\\\"store_true\\\",\\n                   help=\\\"Generate demo data and run bootstrap\\\")\\n    return p.parse_args()\\n\\n\\n# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\\n# Data loading\\n# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\\n\\ndef _reconstruct_episodes(tsr, car, n):\\n    # Fallback: assumes independence between TSR and CAR (per-episode pairing unavailable)\\n    n_success = round(tsr * n)\\n    n_collision = round((1 - car) * n)\\n    success = np.array([1] * n_success + [0] * (n - n_success), dtype=np.int32)\\n    collision = np.array([1] * n_collision + [0] * (n - n_collision), dtype=np.int32)\\n    return success, collision\\n\\n\\ndef load_batch_eval(data, num_episodes=DEFAULT_EPISODES):\\n    \\\"\\\"\\\"Load from batch eval format: {suites: {suite: {tasks: {task_X: {tsr, car}}}}}.\\\"\\\"\\\"\\n    tasks = {}\\n    has_paired = 0\\n    has_unpaired = 0\\n    for suite in SUITE_NAMES:\\n        suite_data = data.get(\\\"suites\\\", {}).get(suite, {})\\n        for tk, tv in suite_data.get(\\\"tasks\\\", {}).items():\\n            if \\\"error\\\" in tv:\\n                continue\\n            if \\\"episodes\\\" in tv and tv[\\\"episodes\\\"]:\\n                episodes = tv[\\\"episodes\\\"]\\n                success = np.array([int(ep[\\\"success\\\"]) for ep in episodes], dtype=np.int32)\\n                collision = np.array([int(ep[\\\"collision\\\"]) for ep in episodes], dtype=np.int32)\\n                has_paired += 1\\n            else:\\n                tsr = tv.get(\\\"tsr\\\", tv.get(\\\"TSR\\\", 0))\\n                car = tv.get(\\\"car\\\", tv.get(\\\"CAR\\\", 0))\\n                success, collision = _reconstruct_episodes(tsr, car, num_episodes)\\n                has_unpaired += 1\\n            tasks[f\\\"{suite}/{tk}\\\"] = {\\n                \\\"description\\\": tv.get(\\\"description\\\", \\\"\\\"),\\n                \\\"success\\\": success,\\n                \\\"collision\\\": collision,\\n                \\\"suite\\\": suite,\\n            }\\n    if has_unpaired > 0:\\n        import sys\\n        print(f\\\"[WARN] {has_unpaired} tasks use reconstructed episodes \\\"\\n              f\\\"(independence assumption, no per-episode pairing). \\\"\\n              f\\\"{has_paired} tasks have paired per-episode data.\\\", file=sys.stderr)\\n    return tasks\\n\\n\\ndef load_runtime_sdf(data):\\n    \\\"\\\"\\\"Load from runtime SDF format: {tasks: {task_X: {episodes: [...]}}}.\\\"\\\"\\\"\\n    suite = lines.append(\"| Level | B-TSR | G-TSR | B-CAR | G-CAR | ΔCAR |\")\n    lines.append(\"|-------|-------|-------|-------|-------|------|\")\n    for lv in sorted(set(list(baseline.get(\"per_level\", {}).keys()) +\n                         list(geosafe.get(\"per_level\", {}).keys()))):\n        bl = baseline[\"per_level\"].get(lv, {})\n        gl = geosafe[\"per_level\"].get(lv, {})\n        if not bl or not gl:\n            continue\n        dcar = (gl.get(\"car\", 0) - bl.get(\"car\", 0)) * 100\n        sign = \"+\" if dcar >= 0 else \"\"\n        lines.append(\n            f\"| {lv} | {bl.get('tsr',0)*100:.1f} | {gl.get('tsr',0)*100:.1f} \"\n            f\"| {bl.get('car',0)*100:.1f} | {gl.get('car',0)*100:.1f} \"\n            f\"| {sign}{dcar:.1f} |\"\n        )\n\n    # AEGIS comparison\n    our_bl_car = baseline[\"overall\"][\"car\"] * 100\n    diff = abs(our_bl_car - AEGIS_BASELINE_CAR)\n    lines.append(\"\")\n    lines.append(\"### AEGIS Baseline Comparison\")\n    lines.append(f\"- AEGIS reported baseline CAR: {AEGIS_BASELINE_CAR:.2f}%\")\n    lines.append(f\"- Our baseline CAR: {our_bl_car:.1f}%\")\n    lines.append(f\"- Deviation: {diff:.1f}pp\")\n    if diff > 10:\n        lines.append(f\"- ⚠️ WARNING: Deviation > 10pp. Possible causes:\")\n        lines.append(f\"  - Different eval episodes/seeds\")\n        lines.append(f\"  - Different safety level car_bl, car_gs = [], [], [], []\n    for suite in SUITE_NAMES:\n        suite_tsr_bl, suite_tsr_gs = [], []\n        suite_car_bl, suite_car_gs = [], []\n        for lv in levels:\n            key = 100)\n            suite_tsr_gs.append(gs.get(\"tsr\", 0) * 100)\n            suite_car_bl.append(bl.get(\"car\", 0) * 100)\n            suite_car_gs.append(gs.get(\"car\", 0) * 100)\n        tsr_bl.append(suite_tsr_bl)\n        tsr_gs.append(suite_tsr_gs)\n        car_bl.append(suite_car_bl)\n        car_gs.append(suite_car_gs)\n\n    return {\n        \"methods\": [\"baseline\", \"geosafe\"],\n        \"suites\": suites,\n        \"levels\": level_ints,\n        \"tsr\": {\"baseline\": tsr_bl, \"geosafe\": tsr_gs},\n        \"car\": {\"baseline\": car_bl, \"geosafe\": args.levels)\n\n    if baseline is None or geosafe is None:\n        print(\"ERROR: Could not load eval results from one or both dirs.\",\n              file=sys.stderr)\n        sys.exit(1)\n\n    result = {\n        \"baseline\": baseline,\n        \"geosafe\": geosafe,\n        \"delta\": {\n            \"overall\": {\n                \"tsr\": geosafe[\"overall\"][\"tsr\"] - (gs_traj, \"GeoSafe\", \"#EE854A\"),\n        ]):\n            ax = axes[row, col]\n            traj = traj_data[\"trajectory\"]\n            if traj.ndim == 2 and traj.shape[1] >= 2:\n                ax.plot(traj[:, 0], traj[:, 1], color=color, linewidth=1.2,\n                        alpha=0.8, zorder=3)\n                ax.plot(traj[0, 0], traj[0, 1], \"o\", color=\"#2CA02C\",\n                        markersize=5, zorder=5)\n                ax.plot(traj[-1, 0], traj[-1, 1], \"s\", color=\"#1A1A1A\",\n                        markersize=4, zorder=5)\n\n                col_steps = traj_data.get(\"collision_steps\", [])\n                if col_steps:\n                    ci = np.array(col_steps)\n                    ci = ci[ci < len(traj)]\n                    if len(ci) > 0:\n spine.set_linewidth(0.4)\n                spine.set_color(\"#CCCCCC\")\n\n    fig.tight_layout()\n    os.makedirs(output_dir, exist_ok=True)\n    pdf_path = os.path.join(output_dir, ### Assistant

_source line 106 · 2026-05-23T01:00:07.255Z_

全部完成。创建的文件：

| 文件 | 大小 | 说明 |
|------|------|------|
| `run_phase2_phase3.sh` | 9.9KB | 主编排脚本，一键执行全部 Phase 2+3 |
| `aggregate_safelibero_results.py` | 12.2KB | 汇总 TSR/CAR/SS（per-suite/level/overall）+ AEGIS 对比 + plot 格式输出 |
| `judge_scenario.py` | 4.7KB | S1-S4 场景判定 + Abstract/下一步推荐 |
| `extract_and_plot_trajectories.py` | 8.7KB | 轨迹提取与可视化（NPZ fallback → JSON fallback → graceful exit） |

与现有脚本的 CLI 适配：
- `measure_inference_latency.py` 用 `--checkpoint` 接口，拆成两次调用（baseline + geosafe），结果对比写入 summary
- `compute_bootstrap_ci.py` 用 `--input` 接口，通过 `find` 定位 JSON 文件路径
- `plot_safelibero_results.py` 用 `--data` 接口，由 aggregate 脚本的 `--plot_output` 生成兼容格式
- `eval_learning_curve.sh` 保持原参数格式 `MODEL_DIR [STEPS] GPU_SERVE GPU_EVAL PORT`
